  354 data QdFpScheme = FastScheme
  355                 | AccurateScheme
  356                 | HighPrecisionScheme
  357   deriving (Show,Eq,Read)
  358 instance Enum QdFpScheme where
  359   succ FastScheme = AccurateScheme
  360   succ AccurateScheme = HighPrecisionScheme
  361   succ HighPrecisionScheme = error "QdFpScheme.succ: HighPrecisionScheme has no successor"
  362 
  363   pred AccurateScheme = FastScheme
  364   pred HighPrecisionScheme = AccurateScheme
  365   pred FastScheme = error "QdFpScheme.pred: FastScheme has no predecessor"
  366 
  367   enumFromTo from to = go from
  368     where
  369       end = fromEnum to
  370       go v = case compare (fromEnum v) end of
  371                  LT -> v : go (succ v)
  372                  EQ -> [v]
  373                  GT -> []
  374 
  375   enumFrom from = enumFromTo from HighPrecisionScheme
  376 
  377   fromEnum FastScheme = 0
  378   fromEnum AccurateScheme = 1
  379   fromEnum HighPrecisionScheme = 2
  380 
  381   toEnum 0 = FastScheme
  382   toEnum 1 = AccurateScheme
  383   toEnum 2 = HighPrecisionScheme
  384   toEnum unmatched = error ("QdFpScheme.toEnum: Cannot match " ++ show unmatched)
  385

  392 data PerpetualFuturesInterpolationType = PerpetualFuturesPiecewiseConstant
  393                                        | PerpetualFuturesLinearInterpolation
  394                                        | PerpetualFuturesCubicSpline
  395   deriving (Show,Eq,Read)
  396 instance Enum PerpetualFuturesInterpolationType where
  397   succ PerpetualFuturesPiecewiseConstant = PerpetualFuturesLinearInterpolation
  398   succ PerpetualFuturesLinearInterpolation = PerpetualFuturesCubicSpline
  399   succ PerpetualFuturesCubicSpline = error "PerpetualFuturesInterpolationType.succ: PerpetualFuturesCubicSpline has no successor"
  400 
  401   pred PerpetualFuturesLinearInterpolation = PerpetualFuturesPiecewiseConstant
  402   pred PerpetualFuturesCubicSpline = PerpetualFuturesLinearInterpolation
  403   pred PerpetualFuturesPiecewiseConstant = error "PerpetualFuturesInterpolationType.pred: PerpetualFuturesPiecewiseConstant has no predecessor"
  404 
  405   enumFromTo from to = go from
  406     where
  407       end = fromEnum to
  408       go v = case compare (fromEnum v) end of
  409                  LT -> v : go (succ v)
  410                  EQ -> [v]
  411                  GT -> []
  412 
  413   enumFrom from = enumFromTo from PerpetualFuturesCubicSpline
  414 
  415   fromEnum PerpetualFuturesPiecewiseConstant = 0
  416   fromEnum PerpetualFuturesLinearInterpolation = 1
  417   fromEnum PerpetualFuturesCubicSpline = 2
  418 
  419   toEnum 0 = PerpetualFuturesPiecewiseConstant
  420   toEnum 1 = PerpetualFuturesLinearInterpolation
  421   toEnum 2 = PerpetualFuturesCubicSpline
  422   toEnum unmatched = error ("PerpetualFuturesInterpolationType.toEnum: Cannot match " ++ show unmatched)

  537 -- |discounts a bond's cash flows off a yield term structure
  538 discountingBondEngine :: (GenYieldTermStructure y) -> (Maybe Bool) -- ^includeSettlementDateFlows
  539  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  540 discountingBondEngine a1 a2 =
  541   withYieldTermStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
  542   let {a2' = fromMaybeBool a2} in 
  543   preErrorCheck $ \a3' -> 
  544   discountingBondEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' >>= \res ->
  545   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  546   errorCheck  a3'>>
  547   return (res')
  548 
  549 
  550 
  551 -- |Discounts perpetual-futures cashflows to the curves' reference date. The
  552 -- three funding vectors must be non-empty and have identical lengths. The
  553 -- engine supports only 'PerpetualFuturesLinear' and 'PerpetualFuturesInverse'
  554 -- payoffs; QuantLib rejects a Quanto payoff at pricing time.
  555 discountingPerpetualFuturesEngine
  556   :: GenYieldTermStructure y1 -> GenYieldTermStructure y2 -> GenQuote q
  557   -> NonEmpty (Double, Double, Double) -- ^@(fundingTime, fundingRate, interestRateDiff)@
  558   -> PerpetualFuturesInterpolationType -> Double -> IO PricingEngine
  559 discountingPerpetualFuturesEngine domestic foreignCurve spot funding interpolation maxT =
  560   qlDiscountingPerpetualFuturesEngine domestic foreignCurve spot times rates diffs interpolation maxT
  561   where (times, rates, diffs) = unzip3 (toList funding)
  562 
  563 qlDiscountingPerpetualFuturesEngine :: (GenYieldTermStructure y1) -- ^domesticDiscountCurve
  564  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y2) -- ^foreignDiscountCurve
  565  -> (GenQuote q) -- ^assetSpot
  566  -> ([Double]) -- ^fundingTimes
  567  -> ([Double]) -- ^fundingRates
  568  -> ([Double]) -- ^interestRateDiffs
  569  -> (PerpetualFuturesInterpolationType) -- ^fundingInterpType
  570  -> (Double) -- ^maxT
  571  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  572 qlDiscountingPerpetualFuturesEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 =
  573   withYieldTermStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
  574   withYieldTermStructure a2 $ \a2' -> 
  575   withQuote a3 $ \a3' -> 
  576   withDoubleArray a4 $ \(a4'1, a4'2) -> 
  577   withDoubleArray a5 $ \(a5'1, a5'2) -> 
  578   withDoubleArray a6 $ \(a6'1, a6'2) -> 
  579   let {a7' = fromEnumC a7} in 
  580   let {a8' = realToFrac a8} in 
  581   preErrorCheck $ \a9' -> 
  582   qlDiscountingPerpetualFuturesEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4'1  a4'2 a5'1  a5'2 a6'1  a6'2 a7' a8' a9' >>= \res ->
  583   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  584   errorCheck  a9'>>
  585   return (res')
  586

  589 -- |discounts a bond's cash flows off a default-risky curve and a flat recovery rate
  590 riskyBondEngine :: (DefaultProbabilityTermStructure) -> (Double) -- ^recoveryRate
  591  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y) -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  592 riskyBondEngine a1 a2 a3 =
  593   withGenTermStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
  594   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
  595   withYieldTermStructure a3 $ \a3' -> 
  596   preErrorCheck $ \a4' -> 
  597   riskyBondEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' >>= \res ->
  598   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  599   errorCheck  a4'>>
  600   return (res')

  604 -- |discounts a swap's legs off a single discount curve
  605 discountingSwapEngine :: (GenYieldTermStructure y) -> (Maybe Bool) -- ^includeSettlementDateFlows
  606  -> (Maybe Day) -- ^settlementDate
  607  -> (Maybe Day) -- ^npvDate
  608  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  609 discountingSwapEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 =
  610   withYieldTermStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
  611   let {a2' = fromMaybeBool a2} in 
  612   withMaybeDay a3 $ \a3' -> 
  613   withMaybeDay a4 $ \a4' -> 
  614   preErrorCheck $ \a5' -> 
  615   discountingSwapEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' >>= \res ->
  616   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  617   errorCheck  a5'>>
  618   return (res')

  622 -- |discounts an FX forward's two legs off their respective currency discount curves
  623 discountingFxForwardEngine :: (GenYieldTermStructure y1) -- ^sourceCurrencyDiscountCurve
  624  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y2) -- ^targetCurrencyDiscountCurve
  625  -> (GenQuote q) -- ^spotFx
  626  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  627 discountingFxForwardEngine a1 a2 a3 =
  628   withYieldTermStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
  629   withYieldTermStructure a2 $ \a2' -> 
  630   withQuote a3 $ \a3' -> 
  631   preErrorCheck $ \a4' -> 
  632   discountingFxForwardEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' >>= \res ->
  633   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  634   errorCheck  a4'>>
  635   return (res')

  639 -- |Discounts each leg of a 'QuantLib.Instrument.Swap.ConstNotionalCrossCurrencySwap' (or either
  640 -- of its two leaves) off its own currency's discount curve, converting to @domesticCcy@ via
  641 -- @spotFX@ (quoted as units of @domesticCcy@ per unit of @foreignCcy@, w.r.t. a settlement equal
  642 -- to the npv date unless @spotFXSettleDate@ says otherwise). Each leg's stored currency must equal
  643 -- @domesticCcy@ or @foreignCcy@; the two discount curves must share the same reference date.
  644 discountingConstNotionalCrossCurrencySwapEngine :: (Currency) -- ^domesticCcy
  645  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y1) -- ^domesticCcyDiscountCurve
  646  -> (Currency) -- ^foreignCcy
  647  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y2) -- ^foreignCcyDiscountCurve
  648  -> (GenQuote q) -- ^spotFX
  649  -> (Maybe Bool) -- ^includeSettlementDateFlows
  650  -> (Maybe Day) -- ^settlementDate
  651  -> (Maybe Day) -- ^npvDate
  652  -> (Maybe Day) -- ^spotFXSettleDate
  653  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  654 discountingConstNotionalCrossCurrencySwapEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 =
  655   withCurrency a1 $ \a1' -> 
  656   withYieldTermStructure a2 $ \a2' -> 
  657   withCurrency a3 $ \a3' -> 
  658   withYieldTermStructure a4 $ \a4' -> 
  659   withQuote a5 $ \a5' -> 
  660   let {a6' = fromMaybeBool a6} in 
  661   withMaybeDay a7 $ \a7' -> 
  662   withMaybeDay a8 $ \a8' -> 
  663   withMaybeDay a9 $ \a9' -> 
  664   preErrorCheck $ \a10' -> 
  665   discountingConstNotionalCrossCurrencySwapEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' >>= \res ->
  666   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  667   errorCheck  a10'>>
  668   return (res')

  672 -- | CVA/DVA-adjusted swap pricing engine. @invstDTS@\/@invstRecoveryRate@ are the
  673 -- own (investor-side) default probability curve and recovery rate for bilateral
  674 -- CVA\/DVA; pass 'Nothing' for @invstDTS@ and @0.999@ for @invstRecoveryRate@ to
  675 -- match upstream's unilateral-CVA-only defaults.
  676 counterpartyAdjSwapEngine :: (GenYieldTermStructure y) -- ^discountCurve
  677  -> (GenQuote q) -- ^blackVol
  678  -> (DefaultProbabilityTermStructure) -- ^ctptyDTS
  679  -> (Double) -- ^ctptyRecoveryRate
  680  -> (Maybe DefaultProbabilityTermStructure) -- ^invstDTS
  681  -> (Double) -- ^invstRecoveryRate
  682  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  683 counterpartyAdjSwapEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 =
  684   withYieldTermStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
  685   withQuote a2 $ \a2' -> 
  686   withGenTermStructure a3 $ \a3' -> 
  687   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
  688   withMaybeDefaultProbabilityTermStructure a5 $ \a5' -> 
  689   let {a6' = realToFrac a6} in 
  690   preErrorCheck $ \a7' -> 
  691   counterpartyAdjSwapEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' >>= \res ->
  692   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  693   errorCheck  a7'>>
  694   return (res')
  695 
  696

  868 -- |/NB/ Timesteps for Cox-Ross-Rubinstein trees are adjusted using the Boyle-Lau algorithm;
  869 -- pass @maxTimeSteps = timeSteps@ to disable it, or @0@ to use the library's default heuristic.
  870 binomialBarrierEngine :: (BinomialTree) -> (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -> (Word) -- ^timeSteps
  871  -> (Word) -- ^maxTimeSteps
  872  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  873 binomialBarrierEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 =
  874   let {a1' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a1} in 
  875   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a2 $ \a2' -> 
  876   let {a3' = fromIntegral a3} in 
  877   let {a4' = fromIntegral a4} in 
  878   preErrorCheck $ \a5' -> 
  879   binomialBarrierEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' >>= \res ->
  880   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  881   errorCheck  a5'>>
  882   return (res')

  886 -- |FX barrier option engine using the vanna-volga method to account for the volatility smile
  887 vannaVolgaBarrierEngine :: (DeltaVolQuote) -- ^atmVol
  888  -> (DeltaVolQuote) -- ^vol25Put
  889  -> (DeltaVolQuote) -- ^vol25Call
  890  -> (GenQuote q) -- ^spotFX
  891  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y1) -- ^domesticTS
  892  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y2) -- ^foreignTS
  893  -> (Bool) -- ^adaptVanDelta
  894  -> (Double) -- ^bsPriceWithSmile
  895  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  896 vannaVolgaBarrierEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 =
  897   withGenQuote a1 $ \a1' -> 
  898   withGenQuote a2 $ \a2' -> 
  899   withGenQuote a3 $ \a3' -> 
  900   withQuote a4 $ \a4' -> 
  901   withYieldTermStructure a5 $ \a5' -> 
  902   withYieldTermStructure a6 $ \a6' -> 
  903   let {a7' = C2HSImp.fromBool a7} in 
  904   let {a8' = realToFrac a8} in 
  905   preErrorCheck $ \a9' -> 
  906   vannaVolgaBarrierEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' >>= \res ->
  907   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  908   errorCheck  a9'>>
  909   return (res')
  910

  927 -- |always uses 'AnalyticDoubleBarrierEngine' as the underlying smile-free double-barrier engine
  928 vannaVolgaDoubleBarrierEngine :: (DeltaVolQuote) -- ^atmVol
  929  -> (DeltaVolQuote) -- ^vol25Put
  930  -> (DeltaVolQuote) -- ^vol25Call
  931  -> (GenQuote q) -- ^spotFX
  932  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y1) -- ^domesticTS
  933  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y2) -- ^foreignTS
  934  -> (Bool) -- ^adaptVanDelta
  935  -> (Double) -- ^bsPriceWithSmile
  936  -> (Int) -- ^series
  937  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  938 vannaVolgaDoubleBarrierEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 =
  939   withGenQuote a1 $ \a1' -> 
  940   withGenQuote a2 $ \a2' -> 
  941   withGenQuote a3 $ \a3' -> 
  942   withQuote a4 $ \a4' -> 
  943   withYieldTermStructure a5 $ \a5' -> 
  944   withYieldTermStructure a6 $ \a6' -> 
  945   let {a7' = C2HSImp.fromBool a7} in 
  946   let {a8' = realToFrac a8} in 
  947   let {a9' = fromIntegral a9} in 
  948   preErrorCheck $ \a10' -> 
  949   vannaVolgaDoubleBarrierEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' >>= \res ->
  950   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  951   errorCheck  a10'>>
  952   return (res')

  971 -- |Monte Carlo pricing engine for double-barrier options
  972 mcDoubleBarrierEngine :: (RngTrait) -> (StatisticsTrait) -> (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -> (Maybe Word) -- ^timeSteps
  973  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^timeStepsPerYear
  974  -> (Bool) -- ^brownianBridge
  975  -> (Bool) -- ^antitheticVariate
  976  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^requiredSamples
  977  -> (Maybe Double) -- ^requiredTolerance
  978  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^maxSamples
  979  -> (Word) -- ^seed
  980  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  981 mcDoubleBarrierEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 a11 =
  982   let {a1' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a1} in 
  983   let {a2' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a2} in 
  984   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a3 $ \a3' -> 
  985   let {a4' = fromMaybeInt a4} in 
  986   let {a5' = fromMaybeInt a5} in 
  987   let {a6' = C2HSImp.fromBool a6} in 
  988   let {a7' = C2HSImp.fromBool a7} in 
  989   let {a8' = fromMaybeInt a8} in 
  990   let {a9' = fromMaybeDouble a9} in 
  991   let {a10' = fromMaybeInt a10} in 
  992   let {a11' = fromIntegral a11} in 
  993   preErrorCheck $ \a12' -> 
  994   mcDoubleBarrierEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' a11' a12' >>= \res ->
  995   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  996   errorCheck  a12'>>
  997   return (res')

 1085 -- |Analytic pricing engine for European continuous geometric average-price Asian options under
 1086 -- the Heston stochastic-volatility model.  Implements the closed-form solution of Kim & Wee,
 1087 -- \"Pricing of geometric Asian options under Heston's stochastic volatility model\", Quantitative
 1088 -- Finance 14:10 (2014). /summationCutoff/ and /xiRightLimit/ bound the truncated summation and
 1089 -- integration ranges used by the closed form; QuantLib's own defaults are 50 and 100.0.
 1090 analyticContinuousGeometricAveragePriceAsianHestonEngine :: (GenHestonProcess hp) -> (Word) -- ^summationCutoff
 1091  -> (Double) -- ^xiRightLimit
 1092  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 1093 analyticContinuousGeometricAveragePriceAsianHestonEngine a1 a2 a3 =
 1094   withHestonProcess a1 $ \a1' -> 
 1095   let {a2' = fromIntegral a2} in 
 1096   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 1097   preErrorCheck $ \a4' -> 
 1098   analyticContinuousGeometricAveragePriceAsianHestonEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' >>= \res ->
 1099   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 1100   errorCheck  a4'>>
 1101   return (res')
 1102

 1105 -- |Analytic pricing engine for European discrete geometric average-price Asian options under the
 1106 -- Heston stochastic-volatility model.  Implements the closed-form solution of Kim, Kim, Kim & Wee,
 1107 -- \"A Recursive Method for Discretely Monitored Geometric Asian Option Prices\", Bull. Korean
 1108 -- Math. Soc. 53 (2016). /xiRightLimit/ bounds the truncated integration range; QuantLib's own
 1109 -- default is 100.0.
 1110 analyticDiscreteGeometricAveragePriceAsianHestonEngine :: (GenHestonProcess hp) -> (Double) -- ^xiRightLimit
 1111  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 1112 analyticDiscreteGeometricAveragePriceAsianHestonEngine a1 a2 =
 1113   withHestonProcess a1 $ \a1' -> 
 1114   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 1115   preErrorCheck $ \a3' -> 
 1116   analyticDiscreteGeometricAveragePriceAsianHestonEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' >>= \res ->
 1117   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 1118   errorCheck  a3'>>
 1119   return (res')

 1123 -- |Monte Carlo pricing engine for continuous fixed-strike lookback options. Exactly one of
 1124 -- @timeSteps@\/@timeStepsPerYear@ must be given; the other must be 'Nothing'.
 1125 mcLookbackFixedEngine :: (RngTrait) -> (StatisticsTrait) -> (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -> (Maybe Word) -- ^timeSteps
 1126  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^timeStepsPerYear
 1127  -> (Bool) -- ^brownianBridge
 1128  -> (Bool) -- ^antitheticVariate
 1129  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^requiredSamples
 1130  -> (Maybe Double) -- ^requiredTolerance
 1131  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^maxSamples
 1132  -> (Word) -- ^seed
 1133  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 1134 mcLookbackFixedEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 a11 =
 1135   let {a1' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a1} in 
 1136   let {a2' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a2} in 
 1137   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a3 $ \a3' -> 
 1138   let {a4' = fromMaybeInt a4} in 
 1139   let {a5' = fromMaybeInt a5} in 
 1140   let {a6' = C2HSImp.fromBool a6} in 
 1141   let {a7' = C2HSImp.fromBool a7} in 
 1142   let {a8' = fromMaybeInt a8} in 
 1143   let {a9' = fromMaybeDouble a9} in 
 1144   let {a10' = fromMaybeInt a10} in 
 1145   let {a11' = fromIntegral a11} in 
 1146   preErrorCheck $ \a12' -> 
 1147   mcLookbackFixedEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' a11' a12' >>= \res ->
 1148   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 1149   errorCheck  a12'>>
 1150   return (res')

 1154 -- |Monte Carlo pricing engine for continuous floating-strike lookback options. Exactly one of
 1155 -- @timeSteps@\/@timeStepsPerYear@ must be given; the other must be 'Nothing'.
 1156 mcLookbackFloatingEngine :: (RngTrait) -> (StatisticsTrait) -> (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -> (Maybe Word) -- ^timeSteps
 1157  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^timeStepsPerYear
 1158  -> (Bool) -- ^brownianBridge
 1159  -> (Bool) -- ^antitheticVariate
 1160  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^requiredSamples
 1161  -> (Maybe Double) -- ^requiredTolerance
 1162  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^maxSamples
 1163  -> (Word) -- ^seed
 1164  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 1165 mcLookbackFloatingEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 a11 =
 1166   let {a1' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a1} in 
 1167   let {a2' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a2} in 
 1168   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a3 $ \a3' -> 
 1169   let {a4' = fromMaybeInt a4} in 
 1170   let {a5' = fromMaybeInt a5} in 
 1171   let {a6' = C2HSImp.fromBool a6} in 
 1172   let {a7' = C2HSImp.fromBool a7} in 
 1173   let {a8' = fromMaybeInt a8} in 
 1174   let {a9' = fromMaybeDouble a9} in 
 1175   let {a10' = fromMaybeInt a10} in 
 1176   let {a11' = fromIntegral a11} in 
 1177   preErrorCheck $ \a12' ->

 1180   errorCheck  a12'>>
 1181   return (res')

 1185 -- |Monte Carlo pricing engine for continuous partial-time fixed-strike lookback options. Exactly
 1186 -- one of @timeSteps@\/@timeStepsPerYear@ must be given; the other must be 'Nothing'.
 1187 mcLookbackPartialFixedEngine :: (RngTrait) -> (StatisticsTrait) -> (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -> (Maybe Word) -- ^timeSteps
 1188  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^timeStepsPerYear
 1189  -> (Bool) -- ^brownianBridge
 1190  -> (Bool) -- ^antitheticVariate
 1191  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^requiredSamples
 1192  -> (Maybe Double) -- ^requiredTolerance
 1193  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^maxSamples
 1194  -> (Word) -- ^seed
 1195  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 1196 mcLookbackPartialFixedEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 a11 =
 1197   let {a1' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a1} in 
 1198   let {a2' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a2} in 
 1199   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a3 $ \a3' -> 
 1200   let {a4' = fromMaybeInt a4} in 
 1201   let {a5' = fromMaybeInt a5} in 
 1202   let {a6' = C2HSImp.fromBool a6} in 
 1203   let {a7' = C2HSImp.fromBool a7} in 
 1204   let {a8' = fromMaybeInt a8} in 
 1205   let {a9' = fromMaybeDouble a9} in 
 1206   let {a10' = fromMaybeInt a10} in 
 1207   let {a11' = fromIntegral a11} in 
 1208   preErrorCheck $ \a12' -> 
 1209   mcLookbackPartialFixedEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' a11' a12' >>= \res ->
 1210   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 1211   errorCheck  a12'>>
 1212   return (res')

 1396  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^timeStepsPerYear

 1398  -> (Bool) -- ^antitheticVariate
 1399  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^requiredSamples
 1400  -> (Maybe Double) -- ^requiredTolerance
 1401  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^maxSamples
 1402  -> (Word) -- ^seed
 1403  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 1404 mcForwardEuropeanBsEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 a11 =
 1405   let {a1' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a1} in

 1408   let {a4' = fromMaybeInt a4} in 
 1409   let {a5' = fromMaybeInt a5} in 
 1410   let {a6' = C2HSImp.fromBool a6} in 
 1411   let {a7' = C2HSImp.fromBool a7} in 
 1412   let {a8' = fromMaybeInt a8} in 
 1413   let {a9' = fromMaybeDouble a9} in 
 1414   let {a10' = fromMaybeInt a10} in 
 1415   let {a11' = fromIntegral a11} in 
 1416   preErrorCheck $ \a12' -> 
 1417   mcForwardEuropeanBsEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' a11' a12' >>= \res ->
 1418   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 1419   errorCheck  a12'>>
 1420   return (res')

 1659 -- |Black-formula YoY inflation cap\/floor engine. The nominal discount curve and the index's
 1660 -- own linked 'QuantLib.TermStructure.Inflation.YoYInflationTermStructure' are separate --
 1661 -- @nominalTermStructure@ discounts cashflows, while the index forecasts them.
 1662 yoyInflationBlackCapFloorEngine :: (GenYoYInflationIndex yidx) -> (YoYOptionletVolatilitySurface) -- ^vol
 1663  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y) -- ^nominalTermStructure
 1664  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 1665 yoyInflationBlackCapFloorEngine a1 a2 a3 =
 1666   withYoYInflationIndex a1 $ \a1' -> 
 1667   withGenVolatilityTermStructure a2 $ \a2' -> 
 1668   withYieldTermStructure a3 $ \a3' -> 
 1669   preErrorCheck $ \a4' -> 
 1670   yoyInflationBlackCapFloorEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' >>= \res ->
 1671   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 1672   errorCheck  a4'>>
 1673   return (res')
 1674 
 1675 
 1676

 1723 -- |Bachelier (normal) swaption engine, taking a flat volatility quote
 1724 bachelierSwaptionEngine :: (GenYieldTermStructure y) -> (GenQuote q) -> (DayCounter) -> (CashAnnuityModel) -- ^model
 1725  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 1726 bachelierSwaptionEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 =
 1727   withYieldTermStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
 1728   withQuote a2 $ \a2' -> 
 1729   withDayCounter a3 $ \a3' -> 
 1730   let {a4' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a4} in 
 1731   preErrorCheck $ \a5' -> 
 1732   bachelierSwaptionEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' >>= \res ->
 1733   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 1734   errorCheck  a5'>>
 1735   return (res')

 1752 -- |analytic European option pricer including stochastic interest rates (Black-Scholes-Merton + Hull-White)
 1753 analyticBsmHullWhiteEngine :: (Double) -> (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -> (HullWhite) -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 1754 analyticBsmHullWhiteEngine a1 a2 a3 =
 1755   let {a1' = realToFrac a1} in 
 1756   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a2 $ \a2' -> 
 1757   withHullWhite a3 $ \a3' -> 
 1758   preErrorCheck $ \a4' -> 
 1759   analyticBsmHullWhiteEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' >>= \res ->
 1760   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 1761   errorCheck  a4'>>
 1762   return (res')

 1779 -- |cap\/floor pricing engine for any one-factor Gaussian short-rate model, evaluated by
 1780 -- integration over the model's state variable. As 'gaussian1dSwaptionEngine', without
 1781 -- 'Probabilities'.
 1782 gaussian1dCapFloorEngine :: (Gaussian1dModel) -> (Int) -- ^integrationPoints
 1783  -> (Double) -- ^stddevs
 1784  -> (Bool) -- ^extrapolatePayoff
 1785  -> (Bool) -- ^flatPayoffExtrapolation
 1786  -> (Maybe (GenYieldTermStructure y)) -- ^discountCurve
 1787  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 1788 gaussian1dCapFloorEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 =
 1789   withStandalone a1 $ \a1' -> 
 1790   let {a2' = fromIntegral a2} in 
 1791   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 1792   let {a4' = C2HSImp.fromBool a4} in 
 1793   let {a5' = C2HSImp.fromBool a5} in 
 1794   withMaybeYieldTermStructure a6 $ \a6' -> 
 1795   preErrorCheck $ \a7' -> 
 1796   gaussian1dCapFloorEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' >>= \res ->
 1797   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 1798   errorCheck  a7'>>
 1799   return (res')

 1979 -- |Semi-analytic Heston engine with either fixed-order or tolerance-based integration.
 1980 analyticHestonEngine :: GenHestonModel hm -> IntegrationControl -> IO PricingEngine
 1981 analyticHestonEngine model control =
 1982   case control of
 1983     IntegrationOrder order -> analyticHestonEngineOrder model order
 1984     IntegrationTolerance tolerance evaluations -> analyticHestonEngineTolerance model tolerance evaluations
 1985 
 1986 -- |The complex-logarithm evaluation formula 'AnalyticHestonEngine' would pick for the given
 1987 -- maturity and Heston parameters when constructed with 'ComplexLogFormula' left to default to
 1988 -- the model's own heuristic (mirrors upstream's asymptotic-characteristic-function threshold).
 1989 analyticHestonEngineOptimalControlVariate :: (Double) -- ^t
 1990  -> (Double) -- ^v0
 1991  -> (Double) -- ^kappa
 1992  -> (Double) -- ^theta
 1993  -> (Double) -- ^sigma
 1994  -> (Double) -- ^rho
 1995  -> (ComplexLogFormula)
 1996 analyticHestonEngineOptimalControlVariate a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 =
 1997   C2HSImp.unsafePerformIO $
 1998   let {a1' = realToFrac a1} in 
 1999   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 2000   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 2001   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 2002   let {a5' = realToFrac a5} in 
 2003   let {a6' = realToFrac a6} in 
 2004   analyticHestonEngineOptimalControlVariate'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' >>= \res ->
 2005   let {res' = (toEnum . fromIntegral) res} in
 2006   return (res')

 2171 -- |American engine based on the QD+ approximation to the exercise boundary. Mainly a good
 2172 -- initial guess for the exercise boundary of 'qdFpAmericanEngine'; usable as a standalone
 2173 -- (lower-accuracy) American pricer on its own.
 2174 qdPlusAmericanEngine :: (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -> (Word) -- ^interpolationPoints, number of Chebyshev nodes used to interpolate the exercise boundary
 2175  -> (SolverType) -- ^solverType, root-finding method used to locate the exercise boundary
 2176  -> (Double) -- ^eps, solver accuracy
 2177  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^maxIter, solver iteration cap; Nothing uses upstream's default
 2178  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 2179 qdPlusAmericanEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 =
 2180   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a1 $ \a1' -> 
 2181   let {a2' = fromIntegral a2} in 
 2182   let {a3' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a3} in 
 2183   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 2184   let {a5' = fromMaybeInt a5} in 
 2185   preErrorCheck $ \a6' -> 
 2186   qdPlusAmericanEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' >>= \res ->
 2187   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 2188   errorCheck  a6'>>
 2189   return (res')
 2190 
 2191 
 2192 
 2193 -- |High performance\/precision American engine based on fixed point iteration for the exercise
 2194 -- boundary (Andersen, Lake and Offengenden 2015; Andersen and Lake 2021). 'QdFpScheme' selects
 2195 -- one of upstream's three built-in 'iterationScheme's ('FastScheme', 'AccurateScheme',
 2196 -- 'HighPrecisionScheme'), trading speed for accuracy.
 2197 qdFpAmericanEngine :: (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -> (QdFpScheme) -- ^iterationScheme
 2198  -> (FixedPointEquation) -- ^fpEquation, which fixed-point formulation of the exercise boundary equation to solve
 2199  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 2200 qdFpAmericanEngine a1 a2 a3 =
 2201   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a1 $ \a1' -> 
 2202   let {a2' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a2} in 
 2203   let {a3' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a3} in 
 2204   preErrorCheck $ \a4' -> 
 2205   qdFpAmericanEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' >>= \res ->
 2206   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 2207   errorCheck  a4'>>
 2208   return (res')

 2212 -- |Vecer (2001) engine for continuous-averaging arithmetic Asian options, replicating the average
 2213 -- by a self-financing strategy in the underlying and solving the resulting PDE on a finite
 2214 -- @[zMin,zMax]@ grid; requires @zMin <= 0 <= zMax@ and @startDate@ no earlier than the evaluation
 2215 -- date (seasoned Asians are not supported). @currentAverage@ is accepted for parity with upstream's
 2216 -- constructor but is not read by the current implementation (only the not-yet-seasoned case is
 2217 -- handled), so 'Nothing' is fine.
 2218 continuousArithmeticAsianVecerEngine :: (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -> (Maybe (GenQuote q)) -- ^currentAverage
 2219  -> (Day) -- ^startDate
 2220  -> (Word) -- ^timeSteps
 2221  -> (Word) -- ^assetSteps
 2222  -> (Double) -- ^zMin
 2223  -> (Double) -- ^zMax
 2224  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 2225 continuousArithmeticAsianVecerEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 =
 2226   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a1 $ \a1' -> 
 2227   withMaybeQuote a2 $ \a2' -> 
 2228   withDay a3 $ \a3' -> 
 2229   let {a4' = fromIntegral a4} in 
 2230   let {a5' = fromIntegral a5} in 
 2231   let {a6' = realToFrac a6} in 
 2232   let {a7' = realToFrac a7} in 
 2233   preErrorCheck $ \a8' -> 
 2234   continuousArithmeticAsianVecerEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' >>= \res ->
 2235   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 2236   errorCheck  a8'>>
 2237   return (res')

 2598 -- |Choi (2018) \"sum of Black-Scholes-Merton models\" pricing engine for a basket option on
 2599 -- multiple underlyings, correlated via @rho@
 2600 choiBasketEngine :: NonEmpty GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess -> Matrix Double -- ^correlation matrix rho
 2601   -> Double -- ^lambda, upstream default: 10.0
 2602   -> Word -- ^maxNrIntegrationSteps, upstream default: unbounded; the C shim takes a 32-bit count
 2603   -> Bool -- ^calcfwdDelta
 2604   -> Bool -- ^controlVariate
 2605   -> IO PricingEngine
 2606 choiBasketEngine ps (Matrix mr mc md) = qlChoiBasketEngine (toList ps) mr mc md
 2607 qlChoiBasketEngine :: ([GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess]) -> (Word) -> (Word) -> ([Double]) -> (Double) -- ^lambda
 2608  -> (Word) -- ^maxNrIntegrationSteps
 2609  -> (Bool) -- ^calcfwdDelta
 2610  -> (Bool) -- ^controlVariate
 2611  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 2612 qlChoiBasketEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 =
 2613   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcessArray a1 $ \(a1'1, a1'2) -> 
 2614   let {a2' = fromIntegral a2} in 
 2615   let {a3' = fromIntegral a3} in 
 2616   withDoubleArrayRaw a4 $ \a4' -> 
 2617   let {a5' = realToFrac a5} in 
 2618   let {a6' = fromIntegral a6} in 
 2619   let {a7' = C2HSImp.fromBool a7} in 
 2620   let {a8' = C2HSImp.fromBool a8} in 
 2621   preErrorCheck $ \a9' -> 
 2622   qlChoiBasketEngine'_ a1'1  a1'2 a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' >>= \res ->
 2623   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 2624   errorCheck  a9'>>
 2625   return (res')

 2649 singleFactorBsmBasketEngine :: NonEmpty GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess
 2650   -> Double -- ^xTol, upstream default: @1e4*QL_EPSILON@
 2651   -> IO PricingEngine

 2653 qlSingleFactorBsmBasketEngine :: ([GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess]) -> (Double) -- ^xTol
 2654  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 2655 qlSingleFactorBsmBasketEngine a1 a2 =
 2656   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcessArray a1 $ \(a1'1, a1'2) -> 
 2657   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in

 2659   qlSingleFactorBsmBasketEngine'_ a1'1  a1'2 a2' a3' >>= \res ->
 2660   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 2661   errorCheck  a3'>>
 2662   return (res')

 2742 -- |Snapshots @rTS@/@fTS@/@fxVolTS@ at construction time (their underlying @shared_ptr@s are copied
 2743 -- out of their handles): a later relink of a 'RelinkableYieldTermStructure' or
 2744 -- 'RelinkableBlackVolTermStructure' passed in here will /not/ be reflected in this 'FdmQuantoHelper'.
 2745 fdmQuantoHelper :: (GenYieldTermStructure y1) -- ^rTS
 2746  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y2) -- ^fTS
 2747  -> (GenBlackVolTermStructure bv) -- ^fxVolTS
 2748  -> (Double) -- ^equityFxCorrelation
 2749  -> (Double) -- ^exchRateATMlevel
 2750  -> IO ((FdmQuantoHelper))
 2751 fdmQuantoHelper a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 =
 2752   withYieldTermStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
 2753   withYieldTermStructure a2 $ \a2' -> 
 2754   withBlackVolTermStructure a3 $ \a3' -> 
 2755   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 2756   let {a5' = realToFrac a5} in 
 2757   preErrorCheck $ \a6' -> 
 2758   fdmQuantoHelper'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' >>= \res ->
 2759   peekFdmQuantoHelper res >>= \res' ->
 2760   errorCheck  a6'>>
 2761   return (res')

 2765 -- |quanto drift adjustment @domesticRate - foreignRate + equityFxCorrelation*equityVol*fxVol@ over @[t1,t2]@
 2766 quantoAdjustment :: (FdmQuantoHelper) -> (Double) -- ^equityVol
 2767  -> (Double) -- ^t1
 2768  -> (Double) -- ^t2
 2769  -> IO ((Double))
 2770 quantoAdjustment a1 a2 a3 a4 =
 2771   withFdmQuantoHelper a1 $ \a1' -> 
 2772   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 2773   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 2774   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 2775   preErrorCheck $ \a5' -> 
 2776   quantoAdjustment'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' >>= \res ->
 2777   let {res' = realToFrac res} in
 2778   errorCheck  a5'>>
 2779   return (res')

 2783 -- |two-dimensional finite-differences Black-Scholes basket-option pricing engine
 2784 fd2dBlackScholesVanillaEngine :: (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -> (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -> (Double) -- ^correlation
 2785  -> (Word) -- ^xGrid, upstream default: 100
 2786  -> (Word) -- ^yGrid, upstream default: 100
 2787  -> (Word) -- ^tGrid, upstream default: 50
 2788  -> (Word) -- ^dampingSteps, upstream default: 0
 2789  -> (FdmScheme) -- ^schemeDesc, upstream default: 'Hundsdorfer'
 2790  -> (Bool) -- ^localVol
 2791  -> (Double) -- ^illegalLocalVolOverwrite, upstream default: @-Null\<Real\>()@
 2792  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 2793 fd2dBlackScholesVanillaEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 =
 2794   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a1 $ \a1' -> 
 2795   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a2 $ \a2' -> 
 2796   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 2797   let {a4' = fromIntegral a4} in 
 2798   let {a5' = fromIntegral a5} in 
 2799   let {a6' = fromIntegral a6} in 
 2800   let {a7' = fromIntegral a7} in 
 2801   withFdmSchemeDesc a8 $ \a8' -> 
 2802   let {a9' = C2HSImp.fromBool a9} in 
 2803   let {a10' = realToFrac a10} in 
 2804   preErrorCheck $ \a11' -> 
 2805   fd2dBlackScholesVanillaEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' a11' >>= \res ->
 2806   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 2807   errorCheck  a11'>>
 2808   return (res')

 2812 -- |n-dimensional finite-differences Black-Scholes basket-option pricing engine, with an explicit
 2813 -- per-axis grid size
 2814 fdndimBlackScholesVanillaEngineAxisGrids :: NonEmpty GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess -> Matrix Double -- ^correlation matrix rho
 2815   -> NonEmpty Word -- ^xGrids, one per underlying
 2816   -> Word -- ^tGrid, upstream default: 50
 2817   -> Word -- ^dampingSteps, upstream default: 0
 2818   -> FdmScheme -- ^schemeDesc, upstream default: 'Douglas'
 2819   -> IO PricingEngine
 2820 fdndimBlackScholesVanillaEngineAxisGrids ps (Matrix mr mc md) xGrids = qlFdndimBlackScholesVanillaEngine (toList ps) mr mc md (toList xGrids)
 2821 qlFdndimBlackScholesVanillaEngine :: ([GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess]) -> (Word) -> (Word) -> ([Double]) -> ([Word]) -> (Word) -- ^tGrid
 2822  -> (Word) -- ^dampingSteps
 2823  -> (FdmScheme) -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 2824 qlFdndimBlackScholesVanillaEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 =
 2825   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcessArray a1 $ \(a1'1, a1'2) -> 
 2826   let {a2' = fromIntegral a2} in 
 2827   let {a3' = fromIntegral a3} in 
 2828   withDoubleArrayRaw a4 $ \a4' -> 
 2829   withIntArray a5 $ \(a5'1, a5'2) -> 
 2830   let {a6' = fromIntegral a6} in 
 2831   let {a7' = fromIntegral a7} in 
 2832   withFdmSchemeDesc a8 $ \a8' -> 
 2833   preErrorCheck $ \a9' -> 
 2834   qlFdndimBlackScholesVanillaEngine'_ a1'1  a1'2 a2' a3' a4' a5'1  a5'2 a6' a7' a8' a9' >>= \res ->
 2835   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 2836   errorCheck  a9'>>
 2837   return (res')

 3030 -- |/NB/ every Monte Carlo engine in this module is C++-templated on both an RNG policy and a
 3031 -- statistics accumulator; all of them (bar 'mcAmericanBasketEngine', see its own doc comment)
 3032 -- take an explicit 'StatisticsTrait' argument for the latter, letting the caller pick
 3033 -- 'Statistics'\/'GaussianStatistics'\/'GeneralStatistics'\/'IncrementalStatistics' instead of being
 3034 -- pinned to upstream's default @Statistics@.
 3035 mcHestonHullWhiteEngine :: (RngTrait) -> (StatisticsTrait) -> (HybridHestonHullWhiteProcess) -> (Maybe Word) -- ^timeSteps
 3036  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^timStepsPerYear
 3037  -> (Bool) -- ^antitheticVariate
 3038  -> (Bool) -- ^controlVariate
 3039  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^requiredSamples
 3040  -> (Maybe Double) -- ^requiredTolerance
 3041  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^maxSamples
 3042  -> (Word) -- ^seed
 3043  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 3044 mcHestonHullWhiteEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 a11 =
 3045   let {a1' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a1} in 
 3046   let {a2' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a2} in 
 3047   withGenStochasticProcess a3 $ \a3' -> 
 3048   let {a4' = fromMaybeInt a4} in 
 3049   let {a5' = fromMaybeInt a5} in 
 3050   let {a6' = C2HSImp.fromBool a6} in 
 3051   let {a7' = C2HSImp.fromBool a7} in 
 3052   let {a8' = fromMaybeInt a8} in 
 3053   let {a9' = fromMaybeDouble a9} in 
 3054   let {a10' = fromMaybeInt a10} in 
 3055   let {a11' = fromIntegral a11} in 
 3056   preErrorCheck $ \a12' -> 
 3057   mcHestonHullWhiteEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' a11' a12' >>= \res ->
 3058   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 3059   errorCheck  a12'>>
 3060   return (res')

 3064 -- |Monte Carlo (least-squares) pricing engine for American options
 3065 mcAmericanEngine :: (RngTrait) -> (StatisticsTrait) -> (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -> (Maybe Word) -> (Maybe Word) -- ^timeStepsPerYear
 3066  -> (Bool) -- ^antitheticVariate
 3067  -> (Bool) -- ^controlVariate
 3068  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^requiredSamples
 3069  -> (Maybe Double) -- ^requiredTolerance
 3070  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^maxSamples
 3071  -> (Word) -- ^seed
 3072  -> (Word) -- ^polynomOrder
 3073  -> (PolynomialType) -> (Maybe Word) -- ^nCalibrationSamples
 3074  -> (Maybe Bool) -- ^antitheticVariateCalibration
 3075  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^seedCalibration
 3076  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 3077 mcAmericanEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 a11 a12 a13 a14 a15 a16 =
 3078   let {a1' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a1} in 
 3079   let {a2' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a2} in 
 3080   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a3 $ \a3' -> 
 3081   let {a4' = fromMaybeInt a4} in 
 3082   let {a5' = fromMaybeInt a5} in 
 3083   let {a6' = C2HSImp.fromBool a6} in 
 3084   let {a7' = C2HSImp.fromBool a7} in 
 3085   let {a8' = fromMaybeInt a8} in 
 3086   let {a9' = fromMaybeDouble a9} in 
 3087   let {a10' = fromMaybeInt a10} in 
 3088   let {a11' = fromIntegral a11} in 
 3089   let {a12' = fromIntegral a12} in 
 3090   let {a13' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a13} in 
 3091   let {a14' = fromMaybeInt a14} in 
 3092   let {a15' = fromMaybeBool a15} in 
 3093   let {a16' = fromMaybeInt a16} in

 3540 -- |Monte Carlo (least-squares) pricing engine for an American 'basketOption'. /NB/ unlike every
 3541 -- other MC engine in this module, this one has no 'StatisticsTrait' parameter: upstream's
 3542 -- @MCAmericanBasketEngine\<RNG\>@ is templated on @RNG@ only -- its base
 3543 -- @MCLongstaffSchwartzEngine\<BasketOption::engine,MultiVariate,RNG\>@ never forwards a second
 3544 -- template argument, so there is no @S@ to expose here (a real upstream limitation, not an
 3545 -- oversight).
 3546 mcAmericanBasketEngine :: (RngTrait) -> (StochasticProcessArray) -> (Maybe Word) -- ^timeSteps
 3547  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^timeStepsPerYear
 3548  -> (Bool) -- ^brownianBridge
 3549  -> (Bool) -- ^antitheticVariate
 3550  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^requiredSamples
 3551  -> (Maybe Double) -- ^requiredTolerance
 3552  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^maxSamples
 3553  -> (Word) -- ^seed
 3554  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^nCalibrationSamples
 3555  -> (Word) -- ^polynomialOrder
 3556  -> (PolynomialType) -- ^polynomialType
 3557  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 3558 mcAmericanBasketEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 a11 a12 a13 =
 3559   let {a1' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a1} in 
 3560   withGenStochasticProcess a2 $ \a2' -> 
 3561   let {a3' = fromMaybeInt a3} in 
 3562   let {a4' = fromMaybeInt a4} in 
 3563   let {a5' = C2HSImp.fromBool a5} in 
 3564   let {a6' = C2HSImp.fromBool a6} in 
 3565   let {a7' = fromMaybeInt a7} in 
 3566   let {a8' = fromMaybeDouble a8} in 
 3567   let {a9' = fromMaybeInt a9} in 
 3568   let {a10' = fromIntegral a10} in 
 3569   let {a11' = fromMaybeInt a11} in 
 3570   let {a12' = fromIntegral a12} in 
 3571   let {a13' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a13} in 
 3572   preErrorCheck $ \a14' -> 
 3573   mcAmericanBasketEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' a11' a12' a13' a14' >>= \res ->
 3574   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 3575   errorCheck  a14'>>
 3576   return (res')
 3577

 3580 -- |Monte Carlo pricing engine for performance (return) options
 3581 mcPerformanceEngine :: (RngTrait) -> (StatisticsTrait) -> (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -> (Bool) -- ^brownianBridge
 3582  -> (Bool) -- ^antitheticVariate
 3583  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^requiredSamples
 3584  -> (Maybe Double) -- ^requiredTolerance
 3585  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^maxSamples
 3586  -> (Word) -- ^seed
 3587  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 3588 mcPerformanceEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 =
 3589   let {a1' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a1} in 
 3590   let {a2' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a2} in 
 3591   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a3 $ \a3' -> 
 3592   let {a4' = C2HSImp.fromBool a4} in 
 3593   let {a5' = C2HSImp.fromBool a5} in 
 3594   let {a6' = fromMaybeInt a6} in 
 3595   let {a7' = fromMaybeDouble a7} in 
 3596   let {a8' = fromMaybeInt a8} in 
 3597   let {a9' = fromIntegral a9} in 
 3598   preErrorCheck $ \a10' -> 
 3599   mcPerformanceEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' >>= \res ->
 3600   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 3601   errorCheck  a10'>>
 3602   return (res')

 3606 -- |variance-swap pricing engine using Monte Carlo simulation (see the note above
 3607 -- 'mcHestonHullWhiteEngine' for the 'StatisticsTrait' parameter shared by every MC engine here).
 3608 mcVarianceSwapEngine :: (RngTrait) -> (StatisticsTrait) -> (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -> (Maybe Word) -- ^timeSteps
 3609  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^timeStepsPerYear
 3610  -> (Bool) -- ^brownianBridge
 3611  -> (Bool) -- ^antitheticVariate
 3612  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^requiredSamples
 3613  -> (Maybe Double) -- ^requiredTolerance
 3614  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^maxSamples
 3615  -> (Word) -- ^seed
 3616  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 3617 mcVarianceSwapEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 a11 =
 3618   let {a1' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a1} in 
 3619   let {a2' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a2} in 
 3620   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a3 $ \a3' -> 
 3621   let {a4' = fromMaybeInt a4} in 
 3622   let {a5' = fromMaybeInt a5} in 
 3623   let {a6' = C2HSImp.fromBool a6} in 
 3624   let {a7' = C2HSImp.fromBool a7} in 
 3625   let {a8' = fromMaybeInt a8} in 
 3626   let {a9' = fromMaybeDouble a9} in 
 3627   let {a10' = fromMaybeInt a10} in 
 3628   let {a11' = fromIntegral a11} in 
 3629   preErrorCheck $ \a12' -> 
 3630   mcVarianceSwapEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' a11' a12' >>= \res ->
 3631   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 3632   errorCheck  a12'>>
 3633   return (res')

 3671 -- |finite-differences Black-Scholes pricing engine for vanilla options
 3672 fdBlackScholesVanillaEngineNoDividends :: (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -> (Word) -- ^timeSteps
 3673  -> (Word) -- ^gridPoints
 3674  -> (Word) -- ^timeDependent
 3675  -> (FdmScheme) -> (Bool) -- ^localVol
 3676  -> (Double) -- ^illegalLocalVolOverwrite
 3677  -> (CashDividendModel) -- ^cashDividendModel

 3683   let {a4' = fromIntegral a4} in 
 3684   withFdmSchemeDesc a5 $ \a5' -> 
 3685   let {a6' = C2HSImp.fromBool a6} in 
 3686   let {a7' = realToFrac a7} in 
 3687   let {a8' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a8} in 
 3688   preErrorCheck $ \a9' -> 
 3689   fdBlackScholesVanillaEngineNoDividends'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' >>= \res ->
 3690   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 3691   errorCheck  a9'>>

 3696 -- |finite-differences Black-Scholes pricing engine for vanilla options, with discrete dividends

 3698  -> (Word) -- ^gridPoints
 3699  -> (Word) -- ^timeDependent
 3700  -> (FdmScheme) -> (Bool) -- ^localVol
 3701  -> (Double) -- ^illegalLocalVolOverwrite
 3702  -> (CashDividendModel) -- ^cashDividendModel
 3703  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 3704 fdBlackScholesVanillaEngineWithDividends a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 =
 3705   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a1 $ \a1' -> 
 3706   withDividendArray a2 $ \(a2'1, a2'2) -> 
 3707   let {a3' = fromIntegral a3} in 
 3708   let {a4' = fromIntegral a4} in 
 3709   let {a5' = fromIntegral a5} in 
 3710   withFdmSchemeDesc a6 $ \a6' -> 
 3711   let {a7' = C2HSImp.fromBool a7} in 
 3712   let {a8' = realToFrac a8} in 
 3713   let {a9' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a9} in

 3715   fdBlackScholesVanillaEngineWithDividends'_ a1' a2'1  a2'2 a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' >>= \res ->
 3716   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 3717   errorCheck  a10'>>
 3718   return (res')

 3999  -> (Double) -- ^mixingFactor, upstream default: 1.0
 4000  -> IO ((PricingEngine))

 4009   withMaybeLocalVolTermStructure a8 $ \a8' -> 
 4010   let {a9' = realToFrac a9} in

 4123  -> ([Dividend]) -- ^dividends
 4124  -> IO ((PricingEngine))

 4128   let {a3' = fromIntegral a3} in

 4133   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 4134   errorCheck  a6'>>
 4135   return (res')

 4498  -> IO ((Double))
 4499 qlBlackCalculatorThetaPerDay a1 a2 a3 =
 4500   withBlackCalculator a1 $ \a1' -> 
 4501   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 4502   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 4503   preErrorCheck $ \a4' -> 
 4504   qlBlackCalculatorThetaPerDay'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' >>= \res ->
 4505   let {res' = realToFrac res} in
 4506   errorCheck  a4'>>
 4507   return (res')

 4567 -- |Black-Scholes-Merton option-price calculator, from the option type and strike directly
 4568 blackScholesCalculator :: (OptionType) -> (Double) -- ^strike
 4569  -> (Double) -- ^spot
 4570  -> (Double) -- ^growth
 4571  -> (Double) -- ^stdDev
 4572  -> (Double) -- ^discount
 4573  -> IO ((BlackScholesCalculator))
 4574 blackScholesCalculator a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 =
 4575   let {a1' = fromEnumC a1} in 
 4576   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 4577   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 4578   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 4579   let {a5' = realToFrac a5} in 
 4580   let {a6' = realToFrac a6} in 
 4581   preErrorCheck $ \a7' -> 
 4582   blackScholesCalculator'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' >>= \res ->
 4583   peekBlackScholesCalculator res >>= \res' ->
 4584   errorCheck  a7'>>
 4585   return (res')
 4586

 4593  -> (Double) -- ^discount
 4594  -> IO ((BlackScholesCalculator))

 4597   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 4598   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 4599   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 4600   let {a5' = realToFrac a5} in 
 4601   preErrorCheck $ \a6' -> 
 4602   blackScholesCalculatorFromPayoff'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' >>= \res ->
 4603   peekBlackScholesCalculator res >>= \res' ->
 4604   errorCheck  a6'>>
 4605   return (res')

 4673 -- |Bachelier (normal-model) analogue of 'BlackCalculator', for options on a rate rather than a
 4674 -- price. No subclass hierarchy upstream, unlike BlackCalculator\/BlackScholesCalculator, so this
 4675 -- is a single leaf type with its own methods rather than a 'GenBlackCalculator' instance.
 4676 bachelierCalculator :: (OptionType) -> (Double) -- ^strike
 4677  -> (Double) -- ^forward
 4678  -> (Double) -- ^stdDev
 4679  -> (Double) -- ^discount
 4680  -> IO ((BachelierCalculator))
 4681 bachelierCalculator a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 =
 4682   let {a1' = fromEnumC a1} in 
 4683   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 4684   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 4685   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 4686   let {a5' = realToFrac a5} in 
 4687   preErrorCheck $ \a6' -> 
 4688   bachelierCalculator'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' >>= \res ->
 4689   peekBachelierCalculator res >>= \res' ->
 4690   errorCheck  a6'>>
 4691   return (res')
 4692 
 4693 
 4694

 4697  -> (Double) -- ^stdDev
 4698  -> (Double) -- ^discount
 4699  -> IO ((BachelierCalculator))
 4700 bachelierCalculatorFromPayoff a1 a2 a3 a4 =
 4701   withStrikedPayoff a1 $ \a1' -> 
 4702   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 4703   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in

 4706   bachelierCalculatorFromPayoff'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' >>= \res ->
 4707   peekBachelierCalculator res >>= \res' ->
 4708   errorCheck  a5'>>
 4709   return (res')

 4977 -- |The common Black and Bachelier option-calculator surface. Operations whose inputs differ
 4978 -- between the models, such as vanna, remain model-qualified functions.
 4979 class HasOptionCalculator a where
 4980   alpha :: a -> IO Double
 4981   beta :: a -> IO Double
 4982   delta :: a -> Double -> IO Double
 4983   deltaForward :: a -> IO Double
 4984   dividendRho :: a -> Double -> IO Double
 4985   elasticity :: a -> Double -> IO Double
 4986   elasticityForward :: a -> IO Double
 4987   gamma :: a -> Double -> IO Double
 4988   gammaForward :: a -> IO Double
 4989   itmAssetProbability :: a -> IO Double
 4990   itmCashProbability :: a -> IO Double
 4991   rho :: a -> Double -> IO Double
 4992   strikeSensitivity :: a -> IO Double
 4993   strikeGamma :: a -> IO Double
 4994   theta :: a -> Double -> Double -> IO Double
 4995   thetaPerDay :: a -> Double -> Double -> IO Double
 4996   value :: a -> IO Double
 4997   vega :: a -> Double -> IO Double
 4998   volga :: a -> Double -> IO Double
 4999 
 5000 instance HasOptionCalculator (GenBlackCalculator bc) where
 5001   alpha = qlBlackCalculatorAlpha
 5002   beta = qlBlackCalculatorBeta
 5003   delta = qlBlackCalculatorDelta
 5004   deltaForward = qlBlackCalculatorDeltaForward
 5005   dividendRho = qlBlackCalculatorDividendRho
 5006   elasticity = qlBlackCalculatorElasticity
 5007   elasticityForward = qlBlackCalculatorElasticityForward
 5008   gamma = qlBlackCalculatorGamma
 5009   gammaForward = qlBlackCalculatorGammaForward
 5010   itmAssetProbability = qlBlackCalculatorItmAssetProbability
 5011   itmCashProbability = qlBlackCalculatorItmCashProbability
 5012   rho = qlBlackCalculatorRho
 5013   strikeSensitivity = qlBlackCalculatorStrikeSensitivity
 5014   strikeGamma = qlBlackCalculatorStrikeGamma
 5015   theta = qlBlackCalculatorTheta
 5016   thetaPerDay = qlBlackCalculatorThetaPerDay
 5017   value = qlBlackCalculatorValue
 5018   vega = qlBlackCalculatorVega
 5019   volga = qlBlackCalculatorVolga
 5020 
 5021 instance HasOptionCalculator BachelierCalculator where
 5022   alpha = qlBachelierCalculatorAlpha
 5023   beta = qlBachelierCalculatorBeta
 5024   delta = qlBachelierCalculatorDelta
 5025   deltaForward = qlBachelierCalculatorDeltaForward
 5026   dividendRho = qlBachelierCalculatorDividendRho
 5027   elasticity = qlBachelierCalculatorElasticity
 5028   elasticityForward = qlBachelierCalculatorElasticityForward
 5029   gamma = qlBachelierCalculatorGamma
 5030   gammaForward = qlBachelierCalculatorGammaForward
 5031   itmAssetProbability = qlBachelierCalculatorItmAssetProbability
 5032   itmCashProbability = qlBachelierCalculatorItmCashProbability
 5033   rho = qlBachelierCalculatorRho
 5034   strikeSensitivity = qlBachelierCalculatorStrikeSensitivity
 5035   strikeGamma = qlBachelierCalculatorStrikeGamma
 5036   theta = qlBachelierCalculatorTheta
 5037   thetaPerDay = qlBachelierCalculatorThetaPerDay
 5038   value = qlBachelierCalculatorValue
 5039   vega = qlBachelierCalculatorVega
 5040   volga = qlBachelierCalculatorVolga
 5041 
 5042 -- |computes the strike given the option's Black-Scholes delta (in an FX-style delta/vol quotation)
 5043 blackDeltaCalculator :: (OptionType) -> (DeltaType) -> (Double) -- ^spot
 5044  -> (Double) -- ^dDiscount (domestic discount factor)
 5045  -> (Double) -- ^fDiscount (foreign discount factor)
 5046  -> (Double) -- ^stdDev
 5047  -> IO ((BlackDeltaCalculator))
 5048 blackDeltaCalculator a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 =
 5049   let {a1' = fromEnumC a1} in 
 5050   let {a2' = fromEnumC a2} in 
 5051   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 5052   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 5053   let {a5' = realToFrac a5} in 
 5054   let {a6' = realToFrac a6} in 
 5055   preErrorCheck $ \a7' -> 
 5056   blackDeltaCalculator'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' >>= \res ->
 5057   peekBlackDeltaCalculator res >>= \res' ->
 5058   errorCheck  a7'>>
 5059   return (res')

 5512 -- |Black 1976 implied standard deviation by the Li-Rational-Substitution solver, started from the Radoicic-Stefanica approximation. Pass 'Nothing' for the guess to let QuantLib pick the starting point.
 5513 blackImpliedStdDevLiRsFromPayoff :: (PlainVanillaPayoff) -> (Double) -- ^forward
 5514  -> (Double) -- ^blackPrice
 5515  -> (Double) -- ^discount
 5516  -> (Double) -- ^displacement
 5517  -> (Maybe Double) -- ^guess
 5518  -> (Double) -- ^omega
 5519  -> (Double) -- ^accuracy
 5520  -> (Word) -- ^maxIterations
 5521  -> IO ((Double))
 5522 blackImpliedStdDevLiRsFromPayoff a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 =
 5523   withPlainVanillaPayoff a1 $ \a1' -> 
 5524   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 5525   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 5526   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 5527   let {a5' = realToFrac a5} in 
 5528   let {a6' = fromMaybeDouble a6} in 
 5529   let {a7' = realToFrac a7} in 
 5530   let {a8' = realToFrac a8} in 
 5531   let {a9' = fromIntegral a9} in

 5540 -- |Black 1976 implied standard deviation by the Li-Rational-Substitution solver, started from the Radoicic-Stefanica approximation. Pass 'Nothing' for the guess to let QuantLib pick the starting point.
 5541 blackImpliedStdDevLiRs :: (OptionType) -> (Double) -- ^strike
 5542  -> (Double) -- ^forward
 5543  -> (Double) -- ^blackPrice
 5544  -> (Double) -- ^discount
 5545  -> (Double) -- ^displacement
 5546  -> (Maybe Double) -- ^guess
 5547  -> (Double) -- ^omega
 5548  -> (Double) -- ^accuracy
 5549  -> (Word) -- ^maxIterations
 5550  -> IO ((Double))
 5551 blackImpliedStdDevLiRs a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 =
 5552   let {a1' = fromEnumC a1} in 
 5553   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 5554   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 5555   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 5556   let {a5' = realToFrac a5} in 
 5557   let {a6' = realToFrac a6} in 
 5558   let {a7' = fromMaybeDouble a7} in 
 5559   let {a8' = realToFrac a8} in 
 5560   let {a9' = realToFrac a9} in 
 5561   let {a10' = fromIntegral a10} in 
 5562   preErrorCheck $ \a11' -> 
 5563   blackImpliedStdDevLiRs'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' a11' >>= \res ->
 5564   let {res' = realToFrac res} in
 5565   errorCheck  a11'>>
 5566   return (res')

 6020 -- |initial guess (alpha, beta, nu, rho) for SABR calibration, per Le Floc'h and Kennedy
 6021 sabrGuess :: (Double) -- ^k_m
 6022  -> (Double) -- ^vol_m
 6023  -> (Double) -- ^k_0
 6024  -> (Double) -- ^vol_0
 6025  -> (Double) -- ^k_p
 6026  -> (Double) -- ^vol_p
 6027  -> (Double) -- ^forward
 6028  -> (Double) -- ^expiryTime
 6029  -> (Double) -- ^beta
 6030  -> (Double) -- ^shift
 6031  -> (VolatilityType) -- ^volatilityType
 6032  -> IO (([Double]))
 6033 sabrGuess a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 a11 =
 6034   let {a1' = realToFrac a1} in 
 6035   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 6036   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 6037   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 6038   let {a5' = realToFrac a5} in 
 6039   let {a6' = realToFrac a6} in 
 6040   let {a7' = realToFrac a7} in 
 6041   let {a8' = realToFrac a8} in 
 6042   let {a9' = realToFrac a9} in 
 6043   let {a10' = realToFrac a10} in 
 6044   let {a11' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a11} in 
 6045   preArray $ \(a12'1, a12'2) -> 
 6046   preErrorCheck $ \a13' -> 
 6047   sabrGuess'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' a11' a12'1  a12'2 a13' >>
 6048   peekDoubleArray  a12'1  a12'2>>= \a12'' -> 
 6049   errorCheck  a13'>>
 6050   return (a12'')
 6051
